{"file_path":"/Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls/tasks/content-collection-api-tasks.md","content":"# Tasks — Content collection API (`content()`)\n\nReworks the Acrolls docs content front door from three hand-keyed Vite globs into one\ndeclarative collection: `content({ loader, schema, filter, config })`. Design borrowed from\nOgygia's content collections; **no Ogygia runtime, dependency, or island model is adopted.**\n\n## Fixed contract decisions (do not re-litigate)\n\nThese were open and are now closed. Every task below assumes them.\n\n1. **Entry identity.** `Entry.id` is the public routeable slug (`DocsContentDocument.slug`).\n   `Entry.key` is the source filesystem key (`guide/intro.md`). Both are exposed; `id` is what\n   `ids()` returns and what `get()` accepts.\n2. **`filter` receives `{ key, data, meta }` — NOT `id`.** The engine\n   (`createDocsContentSource`) is the sole route authority and host `entries[].href` overrides\n   can change a slug, so no trustworthy `id` exists before the engine runs. Filtering happens\n   on inputs, before the engine. (This corrects an earlier draft that passed a guessed `id`.)\n3. **Both sync and async resolution.** `sourceSync()` for eager glob loaders (keeps the\n   existing synchronous host wiring working), `source()` async for custom/remote loaders.\n4. **Schema is Standard Schema (`~standard`), not a Valibot dependency.** Acrolls adds no\n   validation library to its dependency tree; hosts bring Valibot/Zod/Arktype.\n5. **The engine is not modified.** `packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts` — all nav, group, route,\n   breadcrumb, pager, admission, and diagnostic logic — is reused verbatim. This work is a\n   façade plus a loader seam.\n6. **`filter` ≠ `hidden`.** `filter` removes a document from *every* surface including direct\n   URL access. `hidden` keeps its existing meaning (unlisted but routeable, PRODUCT.md\n   behavior 15). Both must continue to work independently.\n\n## Implementation order\n\nT1 → T2 → T3 → (T4, T5, T6 may run in parallel) → T7.\n\n---\n\n# T1 — Core collection module in `@acrolls/docs`\n\n**Objective.** Add a Vite-agnostic `content()` façade over the existing content-source engine,\nadding Standard Schema frontmatter validation, a uniform `filter`, and the unified\n`{ id, key, data, meta, body }` entry shape.\n\n**Repository context.** Monorepo at repo root, pnpm workspaces, Node ≥ 20.19, TypeScript,\nSvelte 5. Package `packages/docs` (`@acrolls/docs`, private, version 0.3.0) is built with\n`svelte-package -i src/lib -o dist`; every file under `src/lib` is published. Tests are Vitest\n(`pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs test`).\n\nThe engine you are wrapping is `packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts`, exporting\n`createDocsContentSource({ config, documents })` where `documents: readonly DocsContentInput[]`\nand `DocsContentInput = { key, metadata?, facts?, load }`. It returns `DocsContentSource` =\n`{ nav, documents, diagnostics, get(v), load(v), entries() }`. `DocsContentDocument` carries\n`{ key, slug, href, title, description, metadata, facts, hidden, order, loader }`.\n\n**Files.**\n- CREATE `packages/docs/src/lib/collection.ts`\n- CREATE `packages/docs/src/lib/collection.test.ts`\n- EDIT `packages/docs/package.json` — add an export subpath\n- EDIT `packages/docs/src/lib/index.ts` — re-export the new public names\n\n**Do NOT edit** `packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts`, `nav.ts`, `nav-path.ts`, or `types.ts`.\n\n## Contract to implement in `collection.ts`\n\nImport engine pieces from `./content.js` (note the `.js` extension — the package uses ESM\nNodeNext-style specifiers throughout).\n\n```ts\n// Standard Schema v1 — structural, no dependency.\nexport interface StandardSchemaV1\u003cInput = unknown, Output = Input\u003e {\n  readonly '~standard': {\n    readonly version: 1;\n    readonly vendor: string;\n    readonly validate: (value: unknown) =\u003e\n      | { readonly value: Output; readonly issues?: undefined }\n      | { readonly issues: ReadonlyArray\u003c{ readonly message: string;\n            readonly path?: ReadonlyArray\u003cPropertyKey | { key: PropertyKey }\u003e }\u003e };\n  };\n}\n```\n\nTypes to export:\n\n- `LoadedDocument\u003cTDocument\u003e = { key: string; data: DocsMetadata; meta?: DocsDocumentFacts;\n  load: () =\u003e Promise\u003cTDocument\u003e }` — one raw document a loader yields.\n- `ContentLoader\u003cTDocument\u003e = { list(): LoadedDocument\u003cTDocument\u003e[] |\n  Promise\u003cLoadedDocument\u003cTDocument\u003e[]\u003e; readonly eager: boolean;\n  live?(): AsyncIterable\u003cnumber | ReadonlyArray\u003cstring\u003e\u003e }`.\n  `eager: true` means `list()` is synchronous and `sourceSync()` is legal. `live?` is a\n  reserved seam — **declare the type, implement nothing.**\n- `Entry\u003cTData, TDocument\u003e = { id; key; data: TData; meta?: DocsDocumentFacts;\n  body: () =\u003e Promise\u003cTDocument\u003e; href: string; hidden: boolean }` — server-side full entry.\n- `EntrySummary\u003cTData\u003e = { id; key; data: TData; href; hidden }` — wire-safe; **must never\n  carry `body` or `meta`.**\n- `Collection\u003cTDocument, TSchema\u003e` = `{ source(): Promise\u003cDocsContentSource\u003cTDocument\u003e\u003e;\n  sourceSync(): DocsContentSource\u003cTDocument\u003e; get(id): Promise\u003cEntry|null\u003e;\n  list\u003cR\u003e(opts?: { map?: (e: EntrySummary) =\u003e R }): Promise\u003cR[]\u003e; ids(): Promise\u003cstring[]\u003e }`.\n\nFunction to export:\n\n```ts\nexport function content\u003cTDocument, TSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = undefined\u003e(\n  options: {\n    loader: ContentLoader\u003cTDocument\u003e;\n    config: DocsContentConfig;\n    schema?: TSchema;\n    filter?: (e: { key: string; data: InferredData; meta?: DocsDocumentFacts }) =\u003e boolean;\n  }\n): Collection\u003cTDocument, TSchema\u003e;\n```\n\nwhere `InferredData = TSchema extends StandardSchemaV1\u003cany, infer O\u003e ? O : DocsMetadata`.\nType inference must work: with a schema present, `e.data.title` is typed and a nonexistent\nfield is a compile error; with no schema, `data` is `DocsMetadata`.\n\n## Required behavior\n\n1. **Pipeline order is: load → validate → filter → engine.** Both validation and filtering\n   operate on `LoadedDocument[]` and produce `DocsContentInput[]`, which is handed to\n   `createDocsContentSource({ config, documents })` untouched.\n2. **Schema validation.** When `schema` is set, run `schema['~standard'].validate(doc.data)`\n   per document.\n   - Success → the validated `result.value` becomes the document's `metadata` passed to the\n     engine (so coercions/defaults from the schema reach nav titles).\n   - Failure → push a diagnostic `{ code: 'ACROLLS_SCHEMA_INVALID', severity: 'error',\n     file: doc.key, message: 'Frontmatter failed schema: ' + issues joined by '; ',\n     remediation: 'Fix the frontmatter fields to match the collection schema.' }`.\n     Include each issue's `path` in the message when present, rendered dotted\n     (e.g. `title: expected string`), because a bare message is not actionable in a 600-file corpus.\n   - On failure, **mode decides admission**: if `options.config.convention?.mode === 'authored'`,\n     drop the document (rejected — consistent with the engine's authored fail-fast contract);\n     otherwise keep it with its raw unvalidated `data` and still surface the diagnostic\n     (migration mode reports, does not reject).\n3. **Filter.** When `filter` is set, drop any document for which it returns false. Filtered\n   documents must not appear in `nav`, `documents`, `get()`, `load()`, `entries()`, `ids()`, or\n   `list()` — verified by test.\n4. **Diagnostics merge.** The returned source must be the engine's source with collection\n   diagnostics prepended: `{ ...engine, diagnostics: [...collectionDiagnostics, ...engine.diagnostics] }`.\n   Preserve every engine method (`get`/`load`/`entries` are own properties on the returned\n   literal, so object spread is safe — confirm this by reading the engine's return statement).\n5. **`sourceSync()`** throws a clear `Error` when `loader.eager !== true`, with message naming\n   `source()` as the async alternative. When eager, it must be fully synchronous (no promise).\n6. **`get(id)`** resolves the source and delegates to the engine's `get()`, which already\n   accepts slug, key, key-without-extension, and href aliases. Map the resulting\n   `DocsContentDocument` to `Entry` (`id: doc.slug`, `body: doc.loader`). Return `null`, not\n   `undefined`, when absent.\n7. **`list()`** returns summaries for non-hidden documents in engine order, applying `map` when\n   supplied. Never include `body`/`meta`.\n8. **`ids()`** returns `documents.map(d =\u003e d.slug)` — all documents including hidden ones, since\n   hidden pages remain routeable and must be prerendered.\n9. **Determinism.** No `Date.now()`, no random, no iteration-order dependence beyond what the\n   engine already guarantees.\n\n## Tests to write in `collection.test.ts`\n\nFollow the existing style of `packages/docs/src/lib/content.test.ts` (Vitest,\n`describe`/`it`/`expect`, hand-built inline document fixtures, no filesystem). Build a fake\neager loader from an inline array — do not use `import.meta.glob` in tests.\n\nCover:\n- `content()` with no schema and no filter produces a source **identical in nav shape** to\n  calling `createDocsContentSource` directly with the same inputs (regression guard proving the\n  façade is transparent).\n- Schema success replaces metadata with validated output (e.g. a schema that defaults\n  `order: 0` affects sort order).\n- Schema failure in `authored` mode: document dropped from `documents`/`nav`/`get`, and one\n  `ACROLLS_SCHEMA_INVALID` diagnostic with the right `file` is present.\n- Schema failure in migration mode (no `convention.mode`): document retained, diagnostic still\n  present.\n- `filter` removes a document from all of `nav`, `documents`, `get()`, `entries()`, `ids()`,\n  `list()`.\n- `filter` and `hidden` are independent: a `hidden: true` document is absent from `nav` and\n  `list()` but still resolvable via `get()` and present in `ids()`.\n- `sourceSync()` throws on a loader with `eager: false`.\n- `list({ map })` output contains no `body` and no `meta` keys.\n- A stub `StandardSchemaV1` implemented inline (do not add Valibot to devDependencies).\n\n## Packaging edits\n\nIn `packages/docs/package.json` `exports`, add alongside `./content`:\n\n```json\n\"./collection\": { \"types\": \"./dist/collection.d.ts\", \"import\": \"./dist/collection.js\" }\n```\n\nIn `packages/docs/src/lib/index.ts`, re-export `content` and every public type above from\n`./collection.js`. **Do not** re-export collection symbols from `content.ts` — `collection.ts`\nimports `content.ts`, and the reverse edge would create an import cycle.\n\n## Acceptance\n\n- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs test` passes, including all new cases.\n- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs check` passes (svelte-check, no type errors).\n- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs build` emits `dist/collection.js` and `dist/collection.d.ts`.\n- `git diff --stat packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts` is empty.\n\n---\n\n# T2 — Vite loaders and shim re-expression in `@acrolls/sveltekit`\n\n**Objective.** Add the glob loader and the custom-source seam, and re-express the existing\n`createAcrollsDocsSource` over `content()` so old and new hosts share exactly one code path.\n\n**Prerequisite.** T1 merged.\n\n**Repository context.** `packages/sveltekit` (`@acrolls/sveltekit`, private, 0.2.0) is built\nwith `tsup src/index.ts --format esm --dts --clean`; checked with `tsc --noEmit`. It depends on\n`@acrolls/docs` and `@acrolls/mdsvex` (workspace). It has **no test setup today** — do not add\none; correctness is proved by T1 tests plus the T4 example build.\n\nRead `packages/sveltekit/src/index.ts` first. It currently holds `createAcrollsDocsSource` plus\nthe private helpers `normalizeGlobPath` and `removeGlobRoot`, and unrelated mdsvex preprocessor\nfactories (leave those alone).\n\n**Files.** EDIT `packages/sveltekit/src/index.ts` only.\n\n## Contract\n\n```ts\nexport function markdownGlob\u003cTDocument\u003e(opts: {\n  /** import.meta.glob('\u003croot\u003e/**\\/*.md', { import: 'default' }) — lazy component modules */\n  body: Record\u003cstring, () =\u003e Promise\u003cTDocument\u003e\u003e;\n  /** import.meta.glob('\u003croot\u003e/**\\/*.md', { eager: true }) — full eager modules */\n  modules: Record\u003cstring, { metadata?: DocsMetadata; __acrollsDocument?: DocsDocumentFacts }\u003e;\n  /** Directory prefix stripped from glob keys, e.g. '../../content' */\n  root: string;\n}): ContentLoader\u003cTDocument\u003e;\n\nexport function customSource\u003cTDocument\u003e(\n  source: Pick\u003cContentLoader\u003cTDocument\u003e, 'list' | 'live'\u003e\n): ContentLoader\u003cTDocument\u003e;\n```\n\n`markdownGlob` returns `{ eager: true, list() { … } }`, iterating `opts.body` entries, stripping\n`root` from each glob key via the existing `removeGlobRoot`/`normalizeGlobPath` helpers, and\nreading **both** `metadata` and `__acrollsDocument` off the single eager module in `opts.modules`\nkeyed by the same glob key. Missing metadata defaults to `{}`; missing facts stays `undefined`.\n\nThis is the deliberate reduction from **three globs to two**. It cannot become one: Vite needs a\nseparate lazy glob to keep document bodies out of the eager graph. Add a short code comment\nstating exactly that, so a future reader does not \"simplify\" it into an eager-only version and\nsilently make every document body eager.\n\n`customSource` returns `{ eager: false, list: source.list, live: source.live }` — the seam for\nCMS/API/database sources. Add a doc comment: the nav/route engine consumes `list()` output, not\nglobs, so a remote source needs no changes to navigation, routing, breadcrumbs, or pager.\n\n## Shim re-expression\n\nRewrite `createAcrollsDocsSource` to build an eager `ContentLoader` from its existing\n`{ modules, metadata, facts, contentRoot }` inputs and return\n`content({ loader, config }).sourceSync()`. Its exported signature, parameter names, return\ntype, and thrown-error messages **must not change** — including the existing\n`Docs glob key \"…\" is outside configured contentRoot \"…\"` error text, which is asserted by CLI\nonboarding detection and documented in READMEs.\n\nMark it `@deprecated` in TSDoc pointing to `content({ loader: markdownGlob(...) })`. Do not\nremove it and do not emit a runtime deprecation warning — hosts on 0.1.x must keep working\nsilently.\n\nRe-export from `@acrolls/docs` (add to the existing export block) so hosts import everything\nfrom one place: `content`, and the types `ContentLoader`, `LoadedDocument`, `Entry`,\n`EntrySummary`, `Collection`, `StandardSchemaV1`.\n\n## Acceptance\n\n- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/sveltekit check` passes.\n- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/sveltekit build` succeeds and `dist/index.d.ts` declares\n  `markdownGlob`, `customSource`, `content`, and the unchanged `createAcrollsDocsSource`.\n- The public signature diff for `createAcrollsDocsSource` is empty (compare `dist/index.d.ts`\n  before and after).\n\n---\n\n# T3 — Public export surface `acrolls/content`\n\n**Objective.** Expose the new API on the public npm package so hosts write\n`import { content, markdownGlob } from 'acrolls/content'`.\n\n**Prerequisite.** T2 merged.\n\n**Repository context.** `packages/acrolls` is the published package (`acrolls`, public). It\ncontains **hand-written one-line re-export shims** in `exports/` — e.g.\n`exports/docs-content.js` is literally `export * from '@acrolls/docs/content';` with a matching\n`.d.ts`. Scoped `@acrolls/*` packages are bundled (`bundledDependencies`) and are not part of\nthe consumer contract; applications install only `acrolls`.\n\n**Files.**\n- CREATE `packages/acrolls/exports/content.js` and `packages/acrolls/exports/content.d.ts`\n- EDIT `packages/acrolls/package.json`\n\n## Contract\n\nBoth shim files contain the same two lines (`.d.ts` mirrors `.js`, matching the existing\nconvention in `exports/docs-content.d.ts`):\n\n```js\nexport * from '@acrolls/docs/collection';\nexport { markdownGlob, customSource } from '@acrolls/sveltekit';\n```\n\nAggregating both is intentional: `content()` is Vite-agnostic and lives in `@acrolls/docs`,\nwhile `markdownGlob` is Vite-specific and lives in `@acrolls/sveltekit`, but the host should not\nhave to know that split.\n\nIn `packages/acrolls/package.json`:\n- Add to `exports`, placed next to `./docs/content`:\n  ```json\n  \"./content\": { \"types\": \"./exports/content.d.ts\", \"import\": \"./exports/content.js\" }\n  ```\n- Add `node --check exports/content.js` to the existing chained `check` script.\n- Leave `files`, `bundledDependencies`, and `dependencies` unchanged (`exports/` is already\n  published wholesale and both scoped packages are already bundled).\n\n## Acceptance\n\n- `pnpm --filter acrolls check` passes.\n- From a built workspace, `node -e \"import('acrolls/content').then(m =\u003e console.log(Object.keys(m)))\"`\n  lists `content`, `markdownGlob`, and `customSource`.\n\n---\n\n# T4 — Migrate the example host\n\n**Objective.** Convert `examples/kit-consumer` to the new API, proving the ergonomics claim and\nexercising schema + filter end to end.\n\n**Prerequisite.** T3 merged.\n\n**Repository context.** `examples/kit-consumer/src/lib/docs/source.ts` currently declares three\n`import.meta.glob` calls over `'../../content/**/*.md'` (lazy `default`, eager `metadata`, eager\n`__acrollsDocument`) and passes them to `createAcrollsDocsSource` with `contentRoot:\n'../../content'` and a `defineDocsConfig({...})` in `authored` mode with `folders` and `entries`\noverrides. The example has an `/acceptance` route that is the canonical smoke surface\n(PRODUCT.md behaviors 64–65) and must keep passing.\n\n**Files.**\n- EDIT `examples/kit-consumer/src/lib/docs/source.ts`\n- EDIT `examples/kit-consumer/package.json` (add `valibot` to `devDependencies`)\n- Possibly ADD one draft fixture under `examples/kit-consumer/src/content/`\n\n## Contract\n\nRewrite `source.ts` to:\n\n```ts\nexport const docs = content({\n  loader: markdownGlob({\n    body: import.meta.glob('../../content/**/*.md', { import: 'default' }),\n    modules: import.meta.glob('../../content/**/*.md', { eager: true }),\n    root: '../../content'\n  }),\n  schema: v.object({\n    title: v.optional(v.string()),\n    description: v.optional(v.string()),\n    order: v.optional(v.number()),\n    hidden: v.optional(v.boolean()),\n    draft: v.optional(v.boolean())\n  }),\n  filter: (e) =\u003e !e.data.draft,\n  config: defineDocsConfig({ /* keep the existing config object verbatim */ })\n}).sourceSync();\n```\n\nKeep `export const docs` as a `DocsContentSource` (via `sourceSync()`) so **no consuming route,\nlayout, or component in the example needs to change**. Verify this by grepping the example for\n`docs.` usages before and after.\n\n`title` must stay `optional` in the schema even though the host is in `authored` mode: the\nengine's own `ACROLLS_TITLE_REQUIRED` admission rule already enforces titles and derives index\ntitles from folders, and a required-title schema would double-reject index pages. Add a comment\nsaying so.\n\nAdd one new content file with `draft: true` in its frontmatter to prove the filter, and assert\nin the acceptance flow (or a brief note in the example README) that its route 404s rather than\nrendering — the point of `filter` over `hidden` is that drafts are not reachable by direct URL.\n\nAdd `valibot` to the example's `devDependencies` only. **Do not** add it to any `packages/*`\nmanifest.\n\n## Acceptance\n\n- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/example-kit build` succeeds.\n- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/example-kit check` passes.\n- The `/acceptance` route still renders code frame, table, callout, and figure together.\n- The docs sidebar, breadcrumbs, and pager are unchanged versus before the migration (compare a\n  serialized `docs.nav` snapshot before and after; it must be byte-identical apart from the new\n  draft file's absence).\n- The draft page's URL returns the host's normal 404.\n\n---\n\n# T5 — CLI onboarding and detection\n\n**Objective.** Teach `acrolls onboard` to emit the new two-glob snippet, and keep `--check`\ndetection working for both old and new hosts.\n\n**Prerequisite.** T3 merged. Coordinate with T4 (the snippet should match what the example does).\n\n**Repository context.** `packages/cli/src/onboarding.ts`:\n- Around **line 93–98**, filesystem detection asserts the host source file contains\n  `createDocsContentSource` or `createAcrollsDocsSource`, plus `import.meta.glob`, plus the\n  content prefix or `contentRoot`.\n- Around **lines 463–470**, the generated-source checkpoint emits the three-glob snippet built\n  with a `${contentGlob}` template.\n\nPRODUCT.md behaviors 46, 49, and 63 apply: `--json` is a versioned plan; checkpoint ordering and\nJSON field meaning are stable user-facing contracts; the generated-source checkpoint must show\nmatching globs, matching prefixes, and typed metadata.\n\n**Files.** EDIT `packages/cli/src/onboarding.ts`, plus the CLI's existing test fixtures/snapshots\nunder `packages/cli` that assert onboarding output.\n\n## Contract\n\n1. **Snippet.** Replace the three-glob snippet with the `content({ loader: markdownGlob(...) })`\n   form, importing from `acrolls/content`. Keep both globs using the identical `${contentGlob}`\n   template so the \"matching globs, matching prefixes\" requirement still holds visibly. Keep the\n   emitted code typed (the existing `as Record\u003c...\u003e` casts or equivalent).\n2. **Detection.** Extend the line-93 predicate to also accept `content(` together with\n   `markdownGlob(`, so a migrated host is recognized as complete. Keep the existing\n   `createDocsContentSource`/`createAcrollsDocsSource` acceptance — `--check` must not regress a\n   host that has not migrated. Keep the `contentRoot`-or-prefix clause, and add `root:` as an\n   accepted marker since `markdownGlob` names the option `root`.\n3. **Caution text.** The generated-source checkpoint's caution should state that the two globs\n   must use the same pattern, and that the eager glob must not be collapsed into the lazy one.\n4. **Versioning.** If checkpoint text or JSON fields change shape, bump the onboarding plan\n   version per behavior 63 rather than silently altering the contract.\n\nDo not change checkpoint ordering, ids, exit-code semantics, or any other checkpoint.\n\n## Acceptance\n\n- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/cli test` passes with updated snapshots.\n- `acrolls onboard --non-interactive --json` emits a plan whose generated-source snippet compiles\n  when pasted into the example host.\n- `acrolls onboard --check` marks the generated-source step complete against **both** the\n  pre-T4 three-glob `source.ts` and the post-T4 `content()` `source.ts`.\n\n---\n\n# T6 — Documentation\n\n**Objective.** Make the new API the documented default while keeping the old one documented as\nsupported-but-deprecated.\n\n**Prerequisite.** T3 merged. Content should match T4's final example code.\n\n**Files.**\n- EDIT `docs/content-authoring.md` (line ~44 shows the old three-glob call)\n- EDIT `packages/sveltekit/README.md` (lines ~35–46 show the old import and call)\n- EDIT `TECH.md` (line ~109 references `createAcrollsDocsSource()` in the pipeline diagram)\n- EDIT `PRODUCT.md` — add behaviors\n- EDIT `docs/frontmatter-contract-spec.md` (line ~12 names `createAcrollsDocsSource`)\n- CHECK `llms.txt` and `docs/README.md` for stale references and update if present\n\n## Contract\n\n1. Lead every integration example with `content({ loader: markdownGlob(...) })` imported from\n   `acrolls/content`. Show `schema` and `filter` in at least one full example.\n2. Keep a short \"Migrating from `createAcrollsDocsSource`\" section showing old → new side by\n   side, and state plainly that the old function still works and is not scheduled for removal.\n3. Document the `filter` vs `hidden` distinction explicitly, since this is the most likely\n   source of user confusion: `filter` = removed everywhere including direct URL; `hidden` =\n   unlisted but still routeable and still prerendered.\n4. Document that schema failures are diagnostics that follow the existing authored/migration\n   mode policy — they are not a new failure model.\n5. Document the custom-source seam (`customSource({ list })`) as the path to CMS/API-backed\n   docs, and state its current limits honestly: it requires the async `source()` (not\n   `sourceSync()`), and `live()` is a reserved type-level seam with **no implementation yet** —\n   do not present live/SSE content as available.\n6. Add to PRODUCT.md, continuing the existing numbered behavior list, behaviors covering: the\n   single-collection declaration, optional typed frontmatter schema with diagnostics under the\n   existing modes, the uniform filter and its distinction from `hidden`, and the pluggable\n   loader seam. Remove \"remote content\" from the Deferred list **only if** T2's `customSource`\n   actually ships; otherwise reword it to note the seam exists while remote sources remain\n   unimplemented.\n\nDo not overstate: this work changes developer ergonomics and adds build-time type safety. It\ndoes not change what a published site's readers receive. Do not describe it as a performance or\nrendering improvement anywhere.\n\n## Acceptance\n\n- No file in `docs/`, `packages/*/README.md`, `TECH.md`, or `llms.txt` presents the three-glob\n  form as the recommended path.\n- Every code sample in the touched docs compiles against the built packages (paste-test at least\n  the primary integration sample).\n- `grep -rn \"createAcrollsDocsSource\" --include=\"*.md\" .` returns only migration/deprecation\n  mentions.\n\n---\n\n# T7 — Full verification pass\n\n**Objective.** Prove the whole change is coherent, non-breaking for existing hosts, and matches\nthe documented success criteria.\n\n**Prerequisite.** T1–T6 merged.\n\n**Files.** None expected. If this task needs source edits, they are bug fixes and must be\nattributed back to the originating task in the commit message.\n\n## Steps\n\n1. Clean build from the repo root: `pnpm install \u0026\u0026 pnpm build`. Must succeed\n   (PRODUCT.md success criterion 1).\n2. `pnpm -r test` and `pnpm -r check` across the workspace.\n3. Example: `pnpm --filter @acrolls/example-kit build`, then confirm `/acceptance` renders code +\n   callout + table + figure together (success criterion 2).\n4. `./packages/cli/dist/index.js validate examples/starter/article.md` exits 0\n   (success criterion 3).\n5. `acrolls studio` opens a local preview for one file (success criterion 4).\n6. **Backward-compatibility proof.** Restore the pre-T4 three-glob `source.ts` in a scratch copy\n   of the example, build it against the new packages, and confirm it still works with no source\n   changes and no new warnings. This is the single most important check in this task — the\n   deprecated path must not have silently broken.\n7. **Nav-parity proof.** Serialize `docs.nav` from the old-style and new-style example sources\n   over the same content set and diff them. Any difference other than the intentionally filtered\n   draft page is a bug.\n8. Confirm `filter`-removed documents are absent from the built output entirely: they must not\n   appear in prerendered routes, the nav JSON, or the client bundle.\n\n## Acceptance\n\nReport results honestly, including any step that was skipped or could not be run in the\nenvironment. If step 6 or 7 fails, do not paper over it — the façade's whole justification is\nthat it is behavior-preserving.\n"}
]]></arguments>

<tool_body><![CDATA[
@@ -0,0 +1,526 @@
+# Tasks — Content collection API (`content()`)
+
+Reworks the Acrolls docs content front door from three hand-keyed Vite globs into one
+declarative collection: `content({ loader, schema, filter, config })`. Design borrowed from
+Ogygia's content collections; **no Ogygia runtime, dependency, or island model is adopted.**
+
+## Fixed contract decisions (do not re-litigate)
+
+These were open and are now closed. Every task below assumes them.
+
+1. **Entry identity.** `Entry.id` is the public routeable slug (`DocsContentDocument.slug`).
+   `Entry.key` is the source filesystem key (`guide/intro.md`). Both are exposed; `id` is what
+   `ids()` returns and what `get()` accepts.
+2. **`filter` receives `{ key, data, meta }` — NOT `id`.** The engine
+   (`createDocsContentSource`) is the sole route authority and host `entries[].href` overrides
+   can change a slug, so no trustworthy `id` exists before the engine runs. Filtering happens
+   on inputs, before the engine. (This corrects an earlier draft that passed a guessed `id`.)
+3. **Both sync and async resolution.** `sourceSync()` for eager glob loaders (keeps the
+   existing synchronous host wiring working), `source()` async for custom/remote loaders.
+4. **Schema is Standard Schema (`~standard`), not a Valibot dependency.** Acrolls adds no
+   validation library to its dependency tree; hosts bring Valibot/Zod/Arktype.
+5. **The engine is not modified.** `packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts` — all nav, group, route,
+   breadcrumb, pager, admission, and diagnostic logic — is reused verbatim. This work is a
+   façade plus a loader seam.
+6. **`filter` ≠ `hidden`.** `filter` removes a document from *every* surface including direct
+   URL access. `hidden` keeps its existing meaning (unlisted but routeable, PRODUCT.md
+   behavior 15). Both must continue to work independently.
+
+## Implementation order
+
+T1 → T2 → T3 → (T4, T5, T6 may run in parallel) → T7.
+
+---
+
+# T1 — Core collection module in `@acrolls/docs`
+
+**Objective.** Add a Vite-agnostic `content()` façade over the existing content-source engine,
+adding Standard Schema frontmatter validation, a uniform `filter`, and the unified
+`{ id, key, data, meta, body }` entry shape.
+
+**Repository context.** Monorepo at repo root, pnpm workspaces, Node ≥ 20.19, TypeScript,
+Svelte 5. Package `packages/docs` (`@acrolls/docs`, private, version 0.3.0) is built with
+`svelte-package -i src/lib -o dist`; every file under `src/lib` is published. Tests are Vitest
+(`pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs test`).
+
+The engine you are wrapping is `packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts`, exporting
+`createDocsContentSource({ config, documents })` where `documents: readonly DocsContentInput[]`
+and `DocsContentInput = { key, metadata?, facts?, load }`. It returns `DocsContentSource` =
+`{ nav, documents, diagnostics, get(v), load(v), entries() }`. `DocsContentDocument` carries
+`{ key, slug, href, title, description, metadata, facts, hidden, order, loader }`.
+
+**Files.**
+- CREATE `packages/docs/src/lib/collection.ts`
+- CREATE `packages/docs/src/lib/collection.test.ts`
+- EDIT `packages/docs/package.json` — add an export subpath
+- EDIT `packages/docs/src/lib/index.ts` — re-export the new public names
+
+**Do NOT edit** `packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts`, `nav.ts`, `nav-path.ts`, or `types.ts`.
+
+## Contract to implement in `collection.ts`
+
+Import engine pieces from `./content.js` (note the `.js` extension — the package uses ESM
+NodeNext-style specifiers throughout).
+
+```ts
+// Standard Schema v1 — structural, no dependency.
+export interface StandardSchemaV1<Input = unknown, Output = Input> {
+  readonly '~standard': {
+    readonly version: 1;
+    readonly vendor: string;
+    readonly validate: (value: unknown) =>
+      | { readonly value: Output; readonly issues?: undefined }
+      | { readonly issues: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly message: string;
+            readonly path?: ReadonlyArray<PropertyKey | { key: PropertyKey }> }> };
+  };
+}
+```
+
+Types to export:
+
+- `LoadedDocument<TDocument> = { key: string; data: DocsMetadata; meta?: DocsDocumentFacts;
+  load: () => Promise<TDocument> }` — one raw document a loader yields.
+- `ContentLoader<TDocument> = { list(): LoadedDocument<TDocument>[] |
+  Promise<LoadedDocument<TDocument>[]>; readonly eager: boolean;
+  live?(): AsyncIterable<number | ReadonlyArray<string>> }`.
+  `eager: true` means `list()` is synchronous and `sourceSync()` is legal. `live?` is a
+  reserved seam — **declare the type, implement nothing.**
+- `Entry<TData, TDocument> = { id; key; data: TData; meta?: DocsDocumentFacts;
+  body: () => Promise<TDocument>; href: string; hidden: boolean }` — server-side full entry.
+- `EntrySummary<TData> = { id; key; data: TData; href; hidden }` — wire-safe; **must never
+  carry `body` or `meta`.**
+- `Collection<TDocument, TSchema>` = `{ source(): Promise<DocsContentSource<TDocument>>;
+  sourceSync(): DocsContentSource<TDocument>; get(id): Promise<Entry|null>;
+  list<R>(opts?: { map?: (e: EntrySummary) => R }): Promise<R[]>; ids(): Promise<string[]> }`.
+
+Function to export:
+
+```ts
+export function content<TDocument, TSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = undefined>(
+  options: {
+    loader: ContentLoader<TDocument>;
+    config: DocsContentConfig;
+    schema?: TSchema;
+    filter?: (e: { key: string; data: InferredData; meta?: DocsDocumentFacts }) => boolean;
+  }
+): Collection<TDocument, TSchema>;
+```
+
+where `InferredData = TSchema extends StandardSchemaV1<any, infer O> ? O : DocsMetadata`.
+Type inference must work: with a schema present, `e.data.title` is typed and a nonexistent
+field is a compile error; with no schema, `data` is `DocsMetadata`.
+
+## Required behavior
+
+1. **Pipeline order is: load → validate → filter → engine.** Both validation and filtering
+   operate on `LoadedDocument[]` and produce `DocsContentInput[]`, which is handed to
+   `createDocsContentSource({ config, documents })` untouched.
+2. **Schema validation.** When `schema` is set, run `schema['~standard'].validate(doc.data)`
+   per document.
+   - Success → the validated `result.value` becomes the document's `metadata` passed to the
+     engine (so coercions/defaults from the schema reach nav titles).
+   - Failure → push a diagnostic `{ code: 'ACROLLS_SCHEMA_INVALID', severity: 'error',
+     file: doc.key, message: 'Frontmatter failed schema: ' + issues joined by '; ',
+     remediation: 'Fix the frontmatter fields to match the collection schema.' }`.
+     Include each issue's `path` in the message when present, rendered dotted
+     (e.g. `title: expected string`), because a bare message is not actionable in a 600-file corpus.
+   - On failure, **mode decides admission**: if `options.config.convention?.mode === 'authored'`,
+     drop the document (rejected — consistent with the engine's authored fail-fast contract);
+     otherwise keep it with its raw unvalidated `data` and still surface the diagnostic
+     (migration mode reports, does not reject).
+3. **Filter.** When `filter` is set, drop any document for which it returns false. Filtered
+   documents must not appear in `nav`, `documents`, `get()`, `load()`, `entries()`, `ids()`, or
+   `list()` — verified by test.
+4. **Diagnostics merge.** The returned source must be the engine's source with collection
+   diagnostics prepended: `{ ...engine, diagnostics: [...collectionDiagnostics, ...engine.diagnostics] }`.
+   Preserve every engine method (`get`/`load`/`entries` are own properties on the returned
+   literal, so object spread is safe — confirm this by reading the engine's return statement).
+5. **`sourceSync()`** throws a clear `Error` when `loader.eager !== true`, with message naming
+   `source()` as the async alternative. When eager, it must be fully synchronous (no promise).
+6. **`get(id)`** resolves the source and delegates to the engine's `get()`, which already
+   accepts slug, key, key-without-extension, and href aliases. Map the resulting
+   `DocsContentDocument` to `Entry` (`id: doc.slug`, `body: doc.loader`). Return `null`, not
+   `undefined`, when absent.
+7. **`list()`** returns summaries for non-hidden documents in engine order, applying `map` when
+   supplied. Never include `body`/`meta`.
+8. **`ids()`** returns `documents.map(d => d.slug)` — all documents including hidden ones, since
+   hidden pages remain routeable and must be prerendered.
+9. **Determinism.** No `Date.now()`, no random, no iteration-order dependence beyond what the
+   engine already guarantees.
+
+## Tests to write in `collection.test.ts`
+
+Follow the existing style of `packages/docs/src/lib/content.test.ts` (Vitest,
+`describe`/`it`/`expect`, hand-built inline document fixtures, no filesystem). Build a fake
+eager loader from an inline array — do not use `import.meta.glob` in tests.
+
+Cover:
+- `content()` with no schema and no filter produces a source **identical in nav shape** to
+  calling `createDocsContentSource` directly with the same inputs (regression guard proving the
+  façade is transparent).
+- Schema success replaces metadata with validated output (e.g. a schema that defaults
+  `order: 0` affects sort order).
+- Schema failure in `authored` mode: document dropped from `documents`/`nav`/`get`, and one
+  `ACROLLS_SCHEMA_INVALID` diagnostic with the right `file` is present.
+- Schema failure in migration mode (no `convention.mode`): document retained, diagnostic still
+  present.
+- `filter` removes a document from all of `nav`, `documents`, `get()`, `entries()`, `ids()`,
+  `list()`.
+- `filter` and `hidden` are independent: a `hidden: true` document is absent from `nav` and
+  `list()` but still resolvable via `get()` and present in `ids()`.
+- `sourceSync()` throws on a loader with `eager: false`.
+- `list({ map })` output contains no `body` and no `meta` keys.
+- A stub `StandardSchemaV1` implemented inline (do not add Valibot to devDependencies).
+
+## Packaging edits
+
+In `packages/docs/package.json` `exports`, add alongside `./content`:
+
+```json
+"./collection": { "types": "./dist/collection.d.ts", "import": "./dist/collection.js" }
+```
+
+In `packages/docs/src/lib/index.ts`, re-export `content` and every public type above from
+`./collection.js`. **Do not** re-export collection symbols from `content.ts` — `collection.ts`
+imports `content.ts`, and the reverse edge would create an import cycle.
+
+## Acceptance
+
+- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs test` passes, including all new cases.
+- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs check` passes (svelte-check, no type errors).
+- `pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs build` emits `dist/collection.js` and `dist/collection.d.ts`.
+- `git diff --stat packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts` is empty.
+
+---
+
+# T2 — Vite loaders and shim re-expression in `@acrolls/sveltekit`
+
+**Objective.** Add the glob loader and the custom-source seam, and re-express the existing
+`createAcrollsDocsSource` over `content()` so old and new hosts share exactly one code path.
+
... (526 lines total)
]]></tool_body>

Written to [tasks/content-collection-api-tasks.md](acrolls/tasks/content-collection-api-tasks.md), matching the conventions of your existing `tasks/*-plan.md` files (implementation order, risks, verification checkpoints).

## The seven tasks

| # | Task | Package | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| T1 | Core `content()` façade + schema + filter + tests | `@acrolls/docs` | — |
| T2 | `markdownGlob()` / `customSource()` + shim re-expression | `@acrolls/sveltekit` | T1 |
| T3 | Public `acrolls/content` export shim | `acrolls` | T2 |
| T4 | Migrate `examples/kit-consumer` | example | T3 |
| T5 | CLI onboarding snippet + `--check` detection | `@acrolls/cli` | T3 |
| T6 | Docs, README, TECH, PRODUCT behaviors | repo-wide | T3 |
| T7 | Full verification incl. backward-compat proof | — | all |

T4/T5/T6 can run in parallel.

## What I decided so agents aren&#39;t blocked

The doc opens with six **fixed contract decisions** — including the two I flagged as open last time. `Entry.id` = public slug (with `key` kept alongside); both `sourceSync()` and async `source()` ship. Each task assumes them, so no agent has to come back and ask.

## Two things I corrected from my own earlier draft

1. **`filter` no longer receives `id`.** My draft passed a `keyToId()` guess, but the engine is the sole route authority and host `entries[].href` overrides can change a slug — so no trustworthy `id` exists before the engine runs. The filter predicate now takes `{ key, data, meta }` only. The task doc flags this explicitly as a correction.

2. **The three-globs→two reduction gets a defensive comment.** Vite can&#39;t get it to one glob without making every document body eager. T2 requires a code comment stating that, so a future reader doesn&#39;t &#34;simplify&#34; it and silently break lazy loading.

## Where I put the real risk

T7 step 6 (build the *old* three-glob `source.ts` against the new packages) and step 7 (diff serialized `docs.nav` old vs. new) are the load-bearing checks — the entire justification for a façade over a rewrite is that it&#39;s behavior-preserving. The task says plainly: if either fails, don&#39;t paper over it.

I also wrote guardrails into T6: the docs must not describe this as a performance or rendering win, because it isn&#39;t — it&#39;s developer ergonomics and build-time type safety. And `live()` must be documented as a type-level seam with no implementation, not as an available feature.

Nothing in `packages/` was modified — only the new task file was added. Want me to start on T1?

Implement T1 exactly as specified. Summary of scope so you can sanity-check the file:
- CREATE `packages/docs/src/lib/collection.ts` — a `content({ loader, config, schema?, filter? })` façade over the existing engine `createDocsContentSource` from `./content.js`, adding Standard Schema (`~standard`) frontmatter validation, a uniform `filter`, a `ContentLoader` seam, and the `Entry`/`EntrySummary` types.

CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS:
- Do NOT modify `packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts`, `nav.ts`, `nav-path.ts`, or `types.ts`. The engine is reused verbatim. `git diff --stat packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts` must be empty when you finish.

VERIFICATION you must run and get passing before reporting done:
1. `cd /Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls && pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs test`
2. `cd /Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls && pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs check`

Nothing else in the repo was touched (`git status --porcelain packages/docs` shows exactly those four; no other package modified).

## (b) Public API surface of `collection.ts`

```ts
export interface StandardSchemaV1<Input = unknown, Output = Input>   // structural, exactly as specced
export type LoadedDocument<TDocument> = { key; data: DocsMetadata; meta?: DocsDocumentFacts; load: () => Promise<TDocument> }
export type ContentLoader<TDocument> = { list(): LoadedDocument<TDocument>[] | Promise<...>; readonly eager: boolean; live?(): AsyncIterable<number | ReadonlyArray<string>> }
export type Entry<TData, TDocument> = { id; key; data: TData; meta?: DocsDocumentFacts; body: () => Promise<TDocument>; href; hidden }
export type EntrySummary<TData> = { id; key; data: TData; href; hidden }
export type InferredData<TSchema> = TSchema extends StandardSchemaV1<never, infer TOutput> ? TOutput : DocsMetadata
export type Collection<TDocument, TSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = undefined> = {
  source(): Promise<DocsContentSource<TDocument>>
  sourceSync(): DocsContentSource<TDocument>
  get(id: string): Promise<Entry<InferredData<TSchema>, TDocument> | null>
  list<TResult = EntrySummary<InferredData<TSchema>>>(options?: { map?: (e: EntrySummary<InferredData<TSchema>>) => TResult }): Promise<TResult[]>
  ids(): Promise<string[]>
}
export type ContentOptions<TDocument, TSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined> = { loader; config: DocsContentConfig; schema?: TSchema; filter?: (e: { key: string; data: InferredData<TSchema>; meta?: DocsDocumentFacts }) => boolean }
export function content<TDocument, TSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = undefined>(options: ContentOptions<TDocument, TSchema>): Collection<TDocument, TSchema>
```

## (c) Verification results

1. `pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs test` — **PASS**. 3 files, 45 tests passed (`collection.test.ts` 15, `content.test.ts` 22, `nav.test.ts` 8). No pre-existing test regressed.
2. `pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs check` — **PASS**. `COMPLETED 306 FILES 0 ERRORS 0 WARNINGS`.
3. `pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs build` — **PASS**. Emits `packages/docs/dist/collection.js` (4079 B) and `dist/collection.d.ts` (5004 B); the `.d.ts` declares `content` plus all types above.
4. `git diff --stat packages/docs/src/lib/content.ts` — **PASS**, empty output. `nav.ts`, `nav-path.ts`, `types.ts` also untouched.

Extra proof of the inference requirement: I temporarily added a probe file using `filter: (e) => e.data.nope !== 'x'` against a `{ title: string }` schema and svelte-check reported `Property 'nope' does not exist on type '{ title: string; }'`. Probe deleted; check re-run clean.

## (d) Ambiguities / small deviations

- **`InferredData` uses `StandardSchemaV1<never, infer TOutput>`, not `<any, …>`.** `Input` is a purely phantom parameter in the structural interface (it appears nowhere in the members), so the two are interchangeable for inference; `never` avoids an `any` in the exported public surface. Verified working in both directions.
- **`ContentOptions` is exported as a named type.** The spec only showed an inline options object. Naming it was necessary to keep the `content()` signature readable and to let T2 reference it; it's additive and harmless. It's also re-exported from `index.ts`.

There is a test (`preserves every engine method…`) that exercises `get`/`load`/`entries` on the merged object.
- **Filter runs *after* validation**, so `filter` sees the validated/coerced `data` (per "pipeline order is load → validate → filter"). In authored mode, schema-invalid documents are dropped before `filter` ever sees them.
- **Filter arg key order** is `{ key, data, meta }` — asserted by a test that `Object.keys()` equals exactly that and contains no `id`, per fixed decision 2.
- `data ?? {}` defensive default was added for loaders that yield undefined frontmatter, even though the type says `data` is required.

No files under `packages/docs/**`, `packages/cli/**`, `examples/**`, or any `.md` were modified.

## (b) Final signatures

```ts
export type AcrollsMarkdownModule = {
  metadata?: DocsMetadata;
  __acrollsDocument?: DocsDocumentFacts;
};
export type MarkdownGlobOptions<TDocument> = {
  body: Record<string, () => Promise<TDocument>>;
  modules: Record<string, AcrollsMarkdownModule>;
  root: string;
};
export function markdownGlob<TDocument>(options: MarkdownGlobOptions<TDocument>): ContentLoader<TDocument>;

export function customSource<TDocument>(
  source: Pick<ContentLoader<TDocument>, 'list' | 'live'>
): ContentLoader<TDocument>;

export function createAcrollsDocsSource<TDocument>(
  options: AcrollsDocsSourceOptions<TDocument>
): DocsContentSource<TDocument>;   // unchanged; now `content({ loader, config }).sourceSync()`
```

`markdownGlob` iterates `options.body`, root-strips via the existing `normalizeGlobPath`/`removeGlobRoot`, and reads both `metadata` (defaulting `{}`) and `__acrollsDocument` (staying `undefined`) off the single eager module. It returns `{ eager: true, list() }` with a synchronous `list()`. The required comment is on the function explaining the two globs cannot be collapsed (a separate lazy glob keeps bodies out of the eager module graph; collapsing silently makes every body eager).

Also re-exported from `@acrolls/docs/collection`: value `content`; types `Collection`, `ContentLoader`, `Entry`, `EntrySummary`, `LoadedDocument`, `StandardSchemaV1`.

## (c) `createAcrollsDocsSource` in `dist/index.d.ts` — before/after

Before (line 35) and after (line 75) are the same string:

```ts
declare function createAcrollsDocsSource<TDocument>(options: AcrollsDocsSourceOptions<TDocument>): DocsContentSource<TDocument>;
```

The full `diff` of before/after `dist/index.d.ts` contains **only additions**: the two new imports/re-export lines, the `AcrollsMarkdownModule`/`MarkdownGlobOptions` types, `markdownGlob`/`customSource` declarations with their TSDoc, the `@deprecated` paragraph appended to the existing TSDoc block, and the new names in the trailing export list. No line of the existing declaration or of `AcrollsDocsSourceOptions` was modified or removed. The `Docs glob key "…" is outside configured contentRoot "…"` message is untouched and still thrown synchronously (`list()` runs inside the synchronous `sourceSync()`), and no runtime deprecation warning was added.

## (d) Verification results — 5/5 pass

1. `pnpm --filter @acrolls/sveltekit check` — **PASS** (tsc --noEmit, no output)
2. `pnpm --filter @acrolls/sveltekit build` — **PASS** (`DTS dist/index.d.ts 5.39 KB`); `dist/index.d.ts` declares `markdownGlob` (L47), `customSource` (L56), `createAcrollsDocsSource` (L75, unchanged), and re-exports `content` (L9)
3. `pnpm --filter acrolls check` — **PASS** (all seven `node --check` including the new `exports/content.js`)
4. Root `pnpm build` succeeded, then from `packages/acrolls`: `node -e "import('acrolls/content')…"` → `[ 'content', 'customSource', 'markdownGlob' ]` — **PASS**
5. `pnpm --filter @acrolls/docs test` — **PASS** (3 files, 45 tests: nav 8, collection 15, content 22)

## (e) Deviations

Two minor, both additive and within contract:

- Exported `AcrollsMarkdownModule` and `MarkdownGlobOptions<TDocument>` as named types rather than inlining the options object. The spec wrote the signature inline; naming them is required for a clean `.d.ts` and gives T4/T5 a type to reference. Structurally identical.
- The shim's inline loader passes `data: options.metadata?.[key] ?? {}` where the old code passed `metadata: undefined`. This is behavior-identical: the engine does `input.metadata ?? {}` at both `content.ts:256` and `content.ts:492`, and `LoadedDocument.data` is non-optional. Verified before making the change.

## (f) Handover to T4 / T5 / T6

The exact import and call form a host should now write:

```ts
import { content, markdownGlob } from 'acrolls/content';
import { defineDocsConfig } from 'acrolls/docs/content';

export const docs = content({
  loader: markdownGlob({
    body: import.meta.glob('../../content/**/*.md', { import: 'default' }),
    modules: import.meta.glob('../../content/**/*.md', { eager: true }),
    root: '../../content'
  }),
  schema: /* optional Standard Schema */,
  filter: (e) => !e.data.draft,
  config: defineDocsConfig({ /* … */ })
}).sourceSync();
```

Points each downstream task needs:

- **Option name is `root`**, not `contentRoot` (T5's detection marker).
- **Both globs must use the identical pattern string**; `body` takes `{ import: 'default' }`, `modules` takes `{ eager: true }` (no `import:` — the whole module is needed so `metadata` and `__acrollsDocument` come off one eager glob). This is the three-globs-to-two reduction: the old separate `__acrollsDocument` glob is gone.
- `sourceSync()` is legal only because `markdownGlob` sets `eager: true`; it returns a plain `DocsContentSource`, so no consuming route/layout/component changes. `customSource` sets `eager: false` and requires `await collection.source()`.
- `filter` receives `{ key, data, meta }` — no `id`.
- `markdownGlob` and `customSource` are also importable from `@acrolls/sveltekit` directly, and `content` from `@acrolls/docs/collection` or `@acrolls/sveltekit` — but hosts should use the single `acrolls/content` front door.
- For T6: `createAcrollsDocsSource` is now marked `@deprecated` in TSDoc, still exported, still works silently, and is internally the same code path as `content()`.agentId: a67f9158c40bfcd5c (use SendMessage with to: 'a67f9158c40bfcd5c', summary: '<5-10 word recap>' to continue this agent)

<tool_body><![CDATA[
You are implementing ONE task in the Acrolls monorepo at `/Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls`.

Your complete specification is task **T4** in `/Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls/tasks/content-collection-api-tasks.md`. READ THAT FILE FIRST — the "Fixed contract decisions" section at the top (all six are binding) and the full "T4 — Migrate the example host" section.

## Prerequisite work already landed (T1/T2/T3)

The new API is built and working. `acrolls/content` resolves and exports `content`, `markdownGlob`, `customSource`. The exact host form to write is:

```ts
import { content, markdownGlob } from 'acrolls/content';
import { defineDocsConfig } from 'acrolls/docs/content';

export const docs = content({
  loader: markdownGlob({
    body: import.meta.glob('../../content/**/*.md', { import: 'default' }),
    modules: import.meta.glob('../../content/**/*.md', { eager: true }),
    root: '../../content'
  }),
  schema: /* optional Standard Schema, e.g. valibot v.object({...}) */,
  filter: (e) => !e.data.draft,
  config: defineDocsConfig({ /* keep existing config verbatim */ })
}).sourceSync();
```

Binding details:
- The option is named `root` (NOT `contentRoot`).
- Both globs MUST use the identical pattern string. `body` takes `{ import: 'default' }`; `modules` takes `{ eager: true }` with NO `import:` key (the whole module is needed so both `metadata` and `__acrollsDocument` come off one eager glob). The old separate `__acrollsDocument` glob is deleted — this is the three-globs-to-two reduction.
- `filter` receives `{ key, data, meta }` — there is NO `id` field.
- `.sourceSync()` returns a plain `DocsContentSource`, exactly what the old `createAcrollsDocsSource` returned.

## Scope

- EDIT `examples/kit-consumer/src/lib/docs/source.ts` — rewrite per above. Keep `export const docs` a `DocsContentSource` via `.sourceSync()` so NO consuming route/layout/component needs to change. Keep the existing `defineDocsConfig({...})` object VERBATIM (title, baseHref, convention authored mode, subtitle, section, folders, entries).
- EDIT `examples/kit-consumer/package.json` — add `valibot` to `devDependencies` ONLY.
- ADD one content file under `examples/kit-consumer/src/content/` with `draft: true` in frontmatter, to prove the filter removes it from every surface including direct URL.

The schema must keep `title` OPTIONAL even though this host is in `authored` mode — the engine's own `ACROLLS_TITLE_REQUIRED` admission rule already enforces titles and derives index titles from folders, so a required-title schema would double-reject index pages. Add a code comment saying exactly that.

## CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS

- Do NOT modify anything under `packages/**`. T1/T2/T3 are done and verified.
- Do NOT modify any `.md` documentation outside the example's own content/README (T6 owns docs).
- Do NOT add valibot to any `packages/*` manifest — example devDependencies only.
- Before you start, `grep -rn "docs\." examples/kit-consumer/src` to inventory how `docs` is consumed, and verify afterward that NONE of those call sites needed changing. Report this explicitly.
- The `/acceptance` route is the canonical smoke surface (PRODUCT.md behaviors 64-65) and must keep rendering code frame + table + callout + figure together.

## VERIFICATION you must run and get passing before reporting done

1. `cd /Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls && pnpm install` (to pick up valibot)
2. `cd /Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls && pnpm --filter @acrolls/example-kit check`
3. `cd /Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls && pnpm --filter @acrolls/example-kit build`
4. **NAV PARITY PROOF (most important).** Before your rewrite, capture the serialized `docs.nav` produced by the OLD three-glob source. After the rewrite, capture it again. Diff them. They MUST be identical except for the absence of your new draft page. Do this with a temporary throwaway script (delete it afterward) — e.g. a small vite/node harness or a temporary vitest file in the example. Report the actual diff output. If they differ in any other way, that is a bug — investigate and report it, do not paper over it.
5. Confirm the draft page's route is absent from the build output (not in prerendered routes, not in nav).

If a verification step fails, fix the cause and re-run. Do not report success on a failing step.

RETURN: (a) exact files created/edited, (b) the final content of the rewritten `source.ts`, (c) the grep inventory of `docs.` call sites and confirmation none changed, (d) the actual nav-parity diff output from step 4, (e) pass/fail of each verification command, (f) confirmation the draft page 404s / is absent from build output, (g) any deviation or anything surprising you found.
]]></tool_body>
<tool_result><![CDATA[
T4 is complete and verified. Two findings need your attention before T7.

## (a) Files created/edited

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `/Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls/examples/kit-consumer/src/lib/docs/source.ts` | Rewritten to the `content({ loader: markdownGlob(...) })` form; three globs → two |
| `/Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls/examples/kit-consumer/package.json` | Added `"valibot": "^1.1.0"` to `devDependencies` only (resolved 1.4.2) |
| `/Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls/examples/kit-consumer/src/content/guides/draft-note.md` | New fixture with `draft: true` |

No `packages/**` file was touched. `grep -rn "valibot" packages/*/package.json` → absent from every package manifest.

## (b) Final `source.ts`

Shown in full above (85 lines). Key points: both globs use the identical pattern string `'../../content/**/*.md'`, `body` takes `{ import: 'default' }`, `modules` takes `{ eager: true }` with no `import:` key, option is `root`, `filter: (entry) => !entry.data.draft`, `defineDocsConfig({...})` preserved verbatim, terminated by `.sourceSync()`. The `ACROLLS_TITLE_REQUIRED` double-reject comment is at lines 8-12.

## (c) `docs.` call-site inventory — zero changes needed

Identical before and after:

```
src/lib/docs/DocumentPage.svelte:6          docs.get(slug)
src/routes/docs/+layout.svelte:10           docs.nav
src/routes/docs/[...slug]/+page.ts:6        docs.documents.filter(...)
src/routes/docs/[...slug]/+page.ts:10       docs.get(slug)
```

Confirmed: no route, layout, or component was edited. `.sourceSync()` returns the same `DocsContentSource` shape.

## (d) Nav-parity diff

Harness: a temporary prerendered `src/routes/navdump/+server.ts` serializing `{ nav, slugs }` through the real Vite/mdsvex pipeline, captured pre-rewrite and post-rewrite, then deleted.

```
=== DIFF (before vs after) ===
exit=0
```

**Byte-identical, zero differences.** Since the draft fixture didn't exist at baseline, an empty diff alone could also mean a broken glob — so I falsified that by temporarily setting `filter: () => true` and rebuilding:

```
+   "id": "page-guides-1b-draft-19-note",
+   "title": "Draft Note",
+   "href": "/docs/guides/draft-note",
+   "slug": "guides/draft-note",
...
+   "guides/draft-note",
```

The draft page is picked up by the new two-glob loader and removed *by the filter specifically*. Filter restored afterward.

## (e) Verification results

| Command | Result |
|---|---|
| `pnpm install` | **PASS** |
| `pnpm --filter @acrolls/example-kit check` | **PASS** — 421 files, 0 errors, 0 warnings |
| `pnpm --filter @acrolls/example-kit build` | **PASS** |
| Nav parity | **PASS** — identical |
| `/acceptance` smoke surface | **PASS** — built chunk contains `acrolls-code-frame`, `acrolls-table-wrap`, `Callout`, `Figure` |

## (f) Draft page absence

Absent from `nav`, absent from `docs.documents` slugs, so `docs.get('guides/draft-note')` returns falsy and `[...slug]/+page.ts` raises the host's normal `error(404, ...)`. No `/docs/guides/draft-note` route reference in any output. Note the example has no global `prerender = true`, so nothing is prerendered at all — "absent from prerendered routes" is vacuously true here rather than independently meaningful.

## (g) Two deviations you need to decide on

**1. `StandardSchemaV1` cannot accept any real schema library — T1 type bug.** T1 declares `validate` returning only the sync branch, but the Standard Schema spec and Valibot both declare `Result | Promise<Result>`. No Valibot/Zod/Arktype schema is structurally assignable, which defeats fixed-contract decision 4. Since `packages/**` is off-limits to me, I narrowed it at the host with a documented `as unknown as StandardSchemaV1<unknown, v.InferOutput<...>>` cast (lines 21-28) — this preserves full inference, so `entry.data.draft` is typed and a nonexistent field is still a compile error. **The proper fix is one line in `packages/docs/src/lib/collection.ts` (~line 20): add `| Promise<...>` to the `validate` return type**, then delete the cast here. This is independently corroborated — a T6 paste-test file that landed in my directory hit the identical error with no cast.

**2. `filter` does not remove content from the bundle — affects T7 step 8.** T7 expects filtered documents absent from the client bundle. They are not: the draft's rendered body is present in both `.svelte-kit/output/server/chunks/source.js` and `.svelte-kit/output/client/_app/immutable/chunks/a2VpLP98.js`. This is inherent to glob loading — `import.meta.glob` materializes every matching file and `filter` runs at runtime afterward, so Rollup can't tree-shake it. It is not a regression from my change. The page is genuinely unroutable and 404s; its bytes are simply still shipped. T7 step 8 should be reworded, or `filter` documented as a routing filter rather than a build-time exclusion — worth flagging to T6 since "removed from every surface" reads as stronger than what ships.

**Concurrency note:** two scratch files from parallel agents appeared inside my scope mid-run — `__t6-paste-test.ts` (moved to scratchpad; it was breaking `check`) and `__t5-snippet-check.ts` (self-cleaned by that agent). Neither is present now and the final `check`/`build` ran against a clean tree.agentId: ae344a8a0adc93b60 (use SendMessage with to: 'ae344a8a0adc93b60', summary: '<5-10 word recap>' to continue this agent)

The new API is built and working. `acrolls/content` resolves and exports `content`, `markdownGlob`, `customSource`. The exact host form the CLI must now emit in its generated-source checkpoint:

```ts
import { content, markdownGlob } from 'acrolls/content';
import { defineDocsConfig } from 'acrolls/docs/content';

export const docs = content({
  loader: markdownGlob({
    body: import.meta.glob('<contentGlob>/**/*.md', { import: 'default' }),
    modules: import.meta.glob('<contentGlob>/**/*.md', { eager: true }),
    root: '<contentGlob>'
  }),
  config: defineDocsConfig({ /* ... */ })
}).sourceSync();
```

Binding details:
- The option is named `root` (NOT `contentRoot`) — this matters for your detection predicate.
- Both globs MUST use the identical `${contentGlob}` template so the existing "matching globs, matching prefixes" requirement stays visibly true. `body` takes `{ import: 'default' }`; `modules` takes `{ eager: true }` with NO `import:` key. The old separate `__acrollsDocument` glob is DELETED — three globs became two.
- `.sourceSync()` returns a plain `DocsContentSource`.
- `createAcrollsDocsSource` still exists, still works, is marked `@deprecated` — hosts that have not migrated must still pass `--check`.

## Scope — `packages/cli` ONLY

Read `packages/cli/src/onboarding.ts` first. Two regions matter:
- **~lines 93-98**: filesystem detection predicate asserting the host source contains `createDocsContentSource` or `createAcrollsDocsSource`, plus `import.meta.glob`, plus the content prefix or `contentRoot`.
- **~lines 463-470**: the generated-source checkpoint emitting the three-glob snippet via a `${contentGlob}` template.

Do:

2. Extend the detection predicate to ALSO accept `content(` together with `markdownGlob(`, and add `root:` as an accepted marker alongside `contentRoot`. CRITICALLY: keep accepting `createDocsContentSource`/`createAcrollsDocsSource` — `--check` must NOT regress a host that has not migrated.

- Do NOT change checkpoint ordering, checkpoint ids, exit-code semantics, or any OTHER checkpoint.
- Do NOT modify anything under `packages/docs/**`, `packages/sveltekit/**`, `packages/acrolls/**`, `examples/**`, or any `.md` documentation. Those are other tasks.

<tool_result><![CDATA[
T5 complete.

## (a) Files edited
- `/Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls/packages/cli/src/onboarding.ts`
- `/Users/amrit/fractalmandala/acrolls/packages/cli/src/onboarding.test.ts`

Nothing outside `packages/cli` was modified (`git status --porcelain packages/cli` shows exactly those two; the `examples/kit-consumer` changes in the tree are the concurrent T4 agent's, not mine).

## (b) Final emitted snippet (verbatim, from `acrolls onboard --json`)

```ts
import type { Component } from 'svelte';
import { content, markdownGlob } from 'acrolls/content';
import { defineDocsConfig, type DocsMetadata } from 'acrolls/docs/content';

type DocsArticle = Component;

const body = import.meta.glob('../../content/**/*.md', { import: 'default' }) as Record<
  string,
  () => Promise<DocsArticle>
>;
const modules = import.meta.glob('../../content/**/*.md', { eager: true }) as Record<
  string,
  { metadata?: DocsMetadata }
>;

export const docs = content({
  loader: markdownGlob({
    body,
    modules,
    root: '../../content'
  }),
  config: defineDocsConfig({
    title: 'Documentation',
    baseHref: '/docs',
    subtitle: 'Generated from Markdown'
  })
}).sourceSync();
```

Both globs come from the same `${contentGlob}` template and `root` reuses it, so "matching globs, matching prefixes" stays visible; the `as Record<...>` casts keep the metadata typed.

New caution text: *"The lazy body glob and the eager modules glob must use the identical pattern, and root must match that same pattern or every route key will be wrong. Do not collapse the eager glob into the lazy one (or drop body and read .default off the eager modules): Vite needs the separate lazy glob to keep document bodies out of the eager module graph, and merging them pulls the entire corpus into the initial bundle. …"*

## (c) Final detection predicate

```ts
// A migrated host uses `content({ loader: markdownGlob({ body, modules, root }) })`; a host that
// has not migrated still uses `createDocsContentSource`/`createAcrollsDocsSource` with an eager
// `import: 'metadata'` glob. Both must be recognized as complete — `--check` must never regress
// an un-migrated host.
// `[(<]` so an explicitly parameterized call — `markdownGlob<DocsArticle>({ ... })` — still matches.
const collectionSourceReady =
    /\bcontent\s*[(<]/.test(sourceSource) &&
    /\bmarkdownGlob\s*[(<]/.test(sourceSource) &&
    sourceSource.includes('body') &&
    sourceSource.includes('modules') &&
    sourceSource.includes('eager: true');
const legacySourceReady =
    (sourceSource.includes('createDocsContentSource') || sourceSource.includes('createAcrollsDocsSource')) &&
    sourceSource.includes("import: 'metadata'") &&
    sourceSource.includes('metadata') &&
    ((sourceSource.includes('key:') && sourceSource.includes('load')) ||
        (sourceSource.includes('modules') && sourceSource.includes('metadata')));
const sourceReady =
    (collectionSourceReady || legacySourceReady) &&
    sourceSource.includes('import.meta.glob') &&
    sourceSource.includes(contentGlob) &&
    (sourceSource.includes(contentPrefix) ||
        sourceSource.includes('contentRoot') ||
        sourceSource.includes('root:'));
```

The old `import: 'metadata'` requirement moved *inside* the legacy branch — it was a top-level AND before, and the new form has no `import: 'metadata'` glob, so leaving it at top level would have made migrated hosts undetectable.

Two throwaway SvelteKit hosts in a temp dir; `old-host` has the three-glob `createAcrollsDocsSource` form (incl. `import: '__acrollsDocument'`), `new-host` has the snippet the CLI itself emits. 94-Create this file. It maps the src/content/ corpus into a serializable Acrolls page tree.
exit: 0
```

Both `[done]`. This is also locked in as a regression test (`marks the generated source complete for both the legacy three-glob and the content() host`), which builds both fixtures in `os.tmpdir()` and feeds the CLI's own emitted snippet back through the predicate.

## Prerequisite work already landed (T1/T2/T3)

The new API is built and working. The exact host form to document everywhere:

```ts
import { content, markdownGlob } from 'acrolls/content';
import { defineDocsConfig } from 'acrolls/docs/content';

export const docs = content({
  loader: markdownGlob({
    body: import.meta.glob('../../content/**/*.md', { import: 'default' }),
    modules: import.meta.glob('../../content/**/*.md', { eager: true }),
    root: '../../content'
  }),
  schema: /* optional Standard Schema — Valibot/Zod/Arktype, host's choice */,
  filter: (e) => !e.data.draft,
  config: defineDocsConfig({ /* ... */ })
}).sourceSync();
```

Binding facts you must document accurately:
- Public front door is `acrolls/content`, exporting `content`, `markdownGlob`, `customSource`.
- Option is named `root` (NOT `contentRoot`).
- Both globs use the IDENTICAL pattern string; `body` takes `{ import: 'default' }`, `modules` takes `{ eager: true }` with no `import:` key. Three globs became TWO. It cannot become one: Vite needs a separate lazy glob to keep document bodies out of the eager module graph.
- `filter` receives `{ key, data, meta }` — NO `id`.
- `.sourceSync()` works only with eager loaders (`markdownGlob`). `customSource` sets `eager: false` and requires `await collection.source()`.

- Schema is Standard Schema (`~standard`) — Acrolls adds NO validation library dependency; hosts bring their own.

## (b) New PRODUCT.md behaviors (66–71)

> **66.** A host declares its documentation corpus as one content collection rather than as a set of hand-keyed build inputs. A single declaration names where documents come from, how they are validated, which of them are published, and how they are configured, and it resolves to the same content source that powers navigation, routes, breadcrumbs, pager order, and static entries. This is a developer-facing ergonomics and type-safety change; it does not change what a reader of a published site receives.
>
> **67.** Frontmatter validation is optional and host-owned. A host may attach any Standard Schema validator to its collection; Acrolls adds no validation library to the host's dependency tree. When a schema is attached, its validated output becomes the metadata the navigation engine sees, so schema defaults and coercions reach titles and ordering, and the published surface is typed at build time.
>
> **68.** Schema validation introduces no new failure model. A document whose frontmatter fails the host schema produces a stable diagnostic naming the file and the offending field, and then follows the corpus policy already in force: authored mode rejects the document exactly like any other admission failure, and migration mode keeps it with its raw frontmatter and reports. Schema diagnostics appear alongside every other corpus diagnostic.
>
> **69.** A collection accepts one uniform filter that decides publication. A filtered document is removed from every surface, including direct URL access: it has no route, no navigation entry, no prerendered output, and no presence in the client bundle. This is distinct from `hidden` (behavior 15), which remains unlisted-but-routeable and still prerendered. A host that wants a document unreachable uses the filter; a host that wants it unlinked but reachable uses `hidden`. The two compose independently.
>
> **70.** The document store is a pluggable seam. The navigation and route engine consumes a loader's document list, not a Vite glob, so a CMS-, database-, or API-backed source needs no changes to navigation, routing, breadcrumbs, or pager. Acrolls ships the seam and a Markdown glob loader; the adapter to any particular remote system remains host-owned. Non-glob loaders resolve asynchronously.
>
> **71.** The previous generated-source helper remains supported. Its signature, behavior, and error messages are unchanged, it emits no runtime deprecation warning, and it resolves through the same code path as the collection API. It is documented as deprecated to point hosts at the newer surface and is not scheduled for removal.

Existing 1–65 were untouched and not renumbered.

**Deferred-list handling:** PRODUCT.md had no "remote content" entry in the Deferred list — the phrase lived in the docs-source **Non-goals** list (line 118). I reworded that in place *and* added a matching Deferred entry, so nothing was deleted:

- Non-goal now reads: "Do not add CMS storage, required search/indexing, or automatic `.svx` discovery in this feature. Remote content is now partially addressed: behavior 70 ships the loader seam a remote source would plug into, but Acrolls still implements no CMS, database, or API source, and no live or streaming content transport. Building that adapter remains host-owned work."
- New Deferred bullet: "Remote content sources. The pluggable loader seam ships (behavior 70), but no CMS, database, or API source implementation does, and the loader's `live()` hook is a reserved type-level seam with no implementation and no live/streaming content behavior behind it."

## (c) `filter` vs `hidden` explanation (from `docs/integrate-sveltekit.md`)

> ### `filter` versus `hidden`
>
> These are different tools and are routinely confused. They compose independently.
>
> | | `filter` (collection option) | `hidden: true` (frontmatter / folder or entry config) |
> |---|---|---|
> | Where it lives | `content({ filter })` | Document frontmatter or `defineDocsConfig` |
> | Sidebar / `DocsNav` | Absent | Absent |
> | `docs.get()` / `docs.load()` | Absent | **Resolves normally** |
> | `entries()` / `ids()` / prerender | Absent | **Present — the page is still built** |
> | Direct URL visit | 404 | **Renders the page** |
> | Breadcrumbs and pager | Absent | Absent from listings, reachable by URL |
>
> **`filter` removes a document from everywhere, including direct URL access.** It runs before the route engine, so a filtered document never becomes a route at all — it is not in the nav JSON, not in the prerendered output, and not in the client bundle. Use it for drafts, for content gated by environment, and for anything that must not be reachable by guessing a URL.
>
> **`hidden` means unlisted, not private.** A hidden page keeps its route, is still prerendered, and still renders for anyone who has the link. Use it for a deliberately unlinked page you still want to ship — release notes for a customer, a deep-link target, an appendix.
>
> `filter` receives `{ key, data, meta }`. There is deliberately no `id`: the route engine is the sole route authority and host `entries[].href` overrides can change a slug, so no trustworthy public id exists before the engine runs. Filter on the source `key` (`guides/install.md`) or on validated frontmatter.

A condensed version is in `docs/content-authoring.md` (author-facing, framed around frontmatter `hidden`), and one-line versions are in `packages/sveltekit/README.md`, `packages/docs/README.md`, `docs/checklist.md`, `llms.txt`, `TECH.md`, and PRODUCT.md behavior 69.

Both T4 and T6 independently hit the **same real defect**, and T4 found a second one. These block T7, so a corrective wave goes first:

1. **`StandardSchemaV1.validate` is missing the `Promise&lt;…&gt;` branch** — the Standard Schema spec declares `Result | Promise&lt;Result&gt;`, so *no* real Valibot/Zod/Arktype schema is assignable. Splitting the fix by file type so the two agents can&#39;t collide: one owns `.ts`, one owns `.md`.
